# LDO-ALWAYS-ON-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Always-on LDO measurement contract

Numerical stimulus settings come from spec.json. Loads are 1/20/100 uA;
external capacitors are 10/100 nF. Edges take 2 us, initial hold is 100 us,
isolated plateaus are 200 us, and maximum sample gap is 50 ns. Burst plateaus
are 20 us with eight pulses and a 200 us tail. DC error and recovery use the
absolute 1.176–1.224 V band; maximum transient excursion is 96 mV from 1.2 V.
Recovery must complete within 100 us. Evaluate both load-edge directions.
Minimum-load DC overhead is at most 2 uA, other DC loads at most 10 uA.
Absolute VREF current is at most 1 uA. Use the signed terminal currents;
overhead = max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0). Preserve KCL within 1 nA.
No startup, protection, noise or yield requirement is included.

Each standalone DC, load profile, line profile or PSRR condition costs one native
case. A full nominal group has three DC cases and three load profiles (six cases).
Line transient and light/heavy-load PSRR at 1 kHz/1 MHz are reference measurements,
not scored. PSRR is 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)) at the regulated operating point.
Line stimulus goes from minimum to maximum VIN and back at heavy load.

Save full waveforms and OP currents. Settling is final entry into the fixed
recovery band that remains there through the observation end. Check initial
hold DC error and maximum post-edge excursion independently. Burst recovery is
checked after the final falling edge. A complete waveform that never recovers
has a null recovery plus its observation lower bound and fails if that bound
exceeds the limit. Truncated or invalid samples are measurement failures.
One submitted circuit must pass all required conditions without rounding.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-08 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.

- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

Bash
# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-08 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-07 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-08 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-07 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-08 | 1 | dc_mid |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- light_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- burst: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- line: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-08 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

/usr/local/bin/atb-client: Python script, ASCII text executable
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

1 # Netlist grammar
2 
3 Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4 Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5 MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6 Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7 SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8 ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9 R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10 Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11 Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.
12 
13 Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:
14

MODEL

I’ll start with a low-current PMOS differential amplifier driving a PMOS pass device, using a matched output divider for 1.2 V regulation. The bias and compensation stay within the stated current and capacitance limits; I’ll validate the netlist before spending measurement cases.

.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Sub-microamp self-biased PMOS reference and mirrored amplifier tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
* PMOS differential input pair: feedback on left, reference on right.
XMPFB n_left VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
XMPREFIN n_drive VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
* NMOS mirror load; n_drive is the pass-gate control node.
XMNLOADREF n_left n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
XMNLOADOUT n_drive n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
* Large PMOS pass element, sized for 100 uA at low dropout.
XMPASS VOUT n_drive VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal high-value divider sets VOUT = 2*VREF with low overhead.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Dominant amplifier-node compensation; 10 pF explicit total.
CCOMP n_drive VSS 10p
.ends DUT

MODEL

The first trial met nominal DC and overhead limits, but load transients were too large and slow. I’m increasing the mirrored amplifier tail current and lengthening its gain devices while keeping quiescent overhead below the light-load ceiling.

ACTION
0:03:28
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Low-current PMOS reference; 4:1 mirror scales the differential-pair tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
* PMOS differential input pair with longer-channel gain devices.
XMPFB n_left VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
XMPREFIN n_drive VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
* NMOS mirror load.
XMNLOADREF n_left n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMNLOADOUT n_drive n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
* PMOS pass element.
XMPASS VOUT n_drive VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal divider sets target at twice VREF.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Compensation on the amplifier output node.
CCOMP n_drive VSS 5p
.ends DUT
EOF

MODEL

The second trial reduced the peak excursion but still missed recovery, especially during the burst. The control node is current limited, so I’m raising its tail-current ratio again and adding a series RC path from the control node to the output to damp the loaded output pole.

ACTION
0:04:38
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* PMOS current reference and 8:1 mirrored differential-pair tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=1
* Long-channel PMOS differential pair for gain.
XMPFB n_left VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
XMPREFIN n_drive VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
* NMOS mirror load.
XMNLOADREF n_left n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMNLOADOUT n_drive n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
* PMOS pass element.
XMPASS VOUT n_drive VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal feedback divider.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Dominant control-node pole and series-RC zero for the output pole.
CCOMP n_drive VSS 3p
RZ n_drive n_z 15meg
CCOMPZ n_z VOUT 10p
.ends DUT
EOF

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import json, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/workspace')
pvts=['SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
expno=9
for pvt in pvts:
 for cl in (1e-8,1e-7):
  eid=f'exp{expno:02d}'
  req={'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt,'cl':cl}
  (root/'request.json').write_text(json.dumps(req))
  cp=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','request.json'],cwd=root,text=True,capture_output=True)
  if cp.returncode not in (0,1):
   print(eid,pvt,cl,'CLI_EXIT',cp.returncode,cp.stderr[:300]); expno+=1; continue
  try:r=json.loads(cp.stdout)
  except Exception as e:
   print(eid,pvt,cl,'BAD_JSON',str(e),cp.stdout[:200]); expno+=1; continue
  Path(f'/tmp/{eid}.json').write_text(json.dumps(r))
  print('\\n',eid,pvt,cl,'ok',r.get('ok'),'revision',r.get('revision'),'cases',r.get('cases_reserved'),'remaining',r.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'))
  fails=[]
  for c in r.get('cases',[]):
   cid=c.get('case_id'); m=c.get('measurements',{})
   if c.get('status')"'!='"'OK': fails.append((cid,'solver',c.get('status'))); continue
   if cid.startswith('dc_'):
    err=abs(m.get('vout_v',0)-1.2)
    lim=2e-6 if cid=='dc_low' else 1e-5
    if err>0.024:fails.append((cid,'dc_error',err))
    if m.get('overhead_a',0)>lim:fails.append((cid,'overhead',m.get('overhead_a'),lim))
    if m.get('vref_abs_a',0)>1e-6:fails.append((cid,'vref',m.get('vref_abs_a')))
    if m.get('kcl_residual_a',0)>1e-9:fails.append((cid,'kcl',m.get('kcl_residual_a')))
    print(' ',cid,'vout',round(m.get('vout_v',0),6),'err_mV',round(err*1e3,3),'oh_uA',round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,3))
   else:
    ih=m.get('initial_hold',{})
    if ih:
     e=max(abs(ih.get('min_v',1.2)-1.2),abs(ih.get('max_v',1.2)-1.2))
     if e>0.024:fails.append((cid,'initial_hold_mV',e*1e3))
    env=m.get('envelope',{})
    if env:
     e=max(env.get('droop_v',0),env.get('overshoot_v',0))
     if e>0.096:fails.append((cid,'envelope_mV',e*1e3))
    wins=m.get('recovery_windows',[])
    for i,w in enumerate(wins):
     rec=w.get('recovery_s')
     if rec is None or rec>1e-4:fails.append((cid,f'recovery{i}_us',None if rec is None else rec*1e6,w.get('recovery_lower_bound_s')))
    print(' ',cid,'env_mV',round(max(env.get('droop_v',0),env.get('overshoot_v',0))*1e3,2),'rec_us',[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w.get('recovery_s')*1e6,2) for w in wins])
  print(' FAILS',fails if fails else 'none')
  expno+=1
PY"

MODEL

The PVT sweep exposed two rail problems in the first amplifier: at 1.4 V cold it could not pull the pass gate low enough, and at 1.8 V hot it could not raise the gate enough. I’m changing the output stage so a small differential pair controls a rail-to-rail NMOS gate driver; this preserves a PMOS pass device and low quiescent bias.

XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1

XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
* PMOS differential pair; the feedback-side branch drives the gain node.
XMPFB n_err VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
XMPREFIN n_ref VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
* NMOS current-mirror load.
XMNLOADREF n_ref n_ref VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMNLOADFB n_err n_ref VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
* Rail-to-rail NMOS gate driver for the PMOS pass device.
XMNGATE n_gate n_err VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
RGPULL VIN n_gate 2.2meg
XMPASS VOUT n_gate VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal feedback divider.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Compensation on the first-stage output.
CCOMP n_err VSS 5p
.ends DUT
EOF

MODEL

The resistor pullup did not shorten the release response enough and pushed light-load current over its limit. I’m replacing it with a small complementary PMOS pullup device so the gate driver can source current only when the error signal calls for pass-device turnoff.

ACTION
0:14:48
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Low-current PMOS reference and 4:1 mirrored differential-pair tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
* Asymmetric PMOS differential pair; feedback branch is 2.5x larger.
XMPFB n_err VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=2
XMPREFIN n_ref VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
* Scaled NMOS mirror balances the asymmetric PMOS pair.
XMNLOADREF n_ref n_ref VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMNLOADFB n_err n_ref VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=25 L=2
* Complementary gate driver for rail-to-rail PMOS-pass control.
XMNGATE n_gate n_err VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1
XMPGATE n_gate n_err VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=2 L=5
XMPASS VOUT n_gate VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal feedback divider.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
CCOMP n_err VSS 1p
.ends DUT
EOF

MODEL

The buffered design has a good corner DC range but unstable 10 nF transients. I’m returning to the single-stage loop that had good nominal recovery and isolating its pass gate through a resistor divider, which should raise its maximum gate voltage at high VIN without adding a second gain stage.

ACTION
0:16:08
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* PMOS reference and 8:1 mirrored PMOS differential-pair tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPTAIL p_tail p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=1
* PMOS differential amplifier with NMOS mirror load.
XMPFB n_left VFB p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
XMPREFIN n_amp VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2
XMNLOADREF n_left n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMNLOADOUT n_amp n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
* Resistive level shift raises the pass-gate range toward VIN.
RAMP n_amp n_gate 5meg
RGPULL VIN n_gate 5meg
XMPASS VOUT n_gate VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal feedback divider.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Damping and compensation from the single-stage amplifier output.
CCOMP n_amp VSS 3p
RZ n_amp n_z 15meg
CCOMPZ n_z VOUT 10p
.ends DUT
EOF

MODEL

The single-stage resistor level shift reduced its control swing too much. I’m keeping the single-stage loop and changing the input-pair ratio: a wider feedback transistor should stay responsive at low VIN, while a narrower reference-side device should let the output branch rise higher at high VIN.

.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* PMOS source followers level-shift the low-voltage inputs upward.
RSHFB VIN vfb_shift 3.3meg
XMPSHFB VSS VFB vfb_shift VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RSHREF VIN vref_shift 3.3meg
XMPSHREF VSS VREF vref_shift VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
* A divided shifted reference biases the NMOS tail current source.
RBTOP vref_shift n_tailbias 10meg
RBBOT n_tailbias VSS 20meg
XMNTAIL n_tail n_tailbias VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=0.42 L=4.2
* NMOS differential pair, with feedback on the left branch.
XMNFB n_left vfb_shift n_tail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
XMNREF n_drive vref_shift n_tail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
* PMOS current-mirror load gives the control node a full VIN-to-VSS swing.
XMPLOADREF n_left n_left VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
XMPLOADOUT n_drive n_left VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
* PMOS pass device.
XMPASS VOUT n_drive VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
* Equal feedback divider.
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Single-stage compensation.
CCOMP n_drive VSS 3p
RZ n_drive n_z 15meg
CCOMPZ n_z VOUT 10p
.ends DUT

The shifted NMOS input experiment stalled near VIN, so I’m returning to the proven PMOS differential stage. I’ll add a small PMOS pullup controlled by its mirrored branch node, giving the same stage an active high-side path to turn the pass device off near high VIN.

0:21:52

.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* PMOS current reference and 8:1 mirrored differential-pair tail.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1

XMPREFIN n_drive VREF p_tail VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2

XMNLOADOUT n_drive n_left VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
* Small signal-controlled VIN pullup extends the pass-gate high swing.
XMPUP n_drive n_left VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=0.42 L=10

RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
* Single-stage compensation.

MODEL

The shifted input network is burning current without controlling the pass gate, so I’m replacing it with a simpler NMOS differential pair and a mirrored NMOS tail bias. Its PMOS mirror load provides the full gate swing directly.

ACTION
0:23:39
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* PMOS resistor-biased reference mirrored into an NMOS diode bias.
XMPREF p_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
RBIAS p_bias VSS 2.7meg
XMPBIAS n_bias p_bias VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
XMNBREF n_bias n_bias VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=5 L=1
* NMOS tail current mirror.
XMNTAIL n_tail n_bias VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
* NMOS differential pair with direct low-voltage inputs.
XMNFB n_left VFB n_tail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
XMNREF n_drive VREF n_tail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1
* PMOS current-mirror load produces a full-swing pass-gate control.
XMPLOADREF n_left n_left VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMPLOADOUT n_drive n_left VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=2
XMPASS VOUT n_drive VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15
RFBUP VOUT VFB 2.7meg
RFBDN VFB VSS 2.7meg
CCOMP n_drive VSS 3p
RZ n_drive n_z 15meg
CCOMPZ n_z VOUT 10p
.ends DUT
